Scalable, Transferable Meta-network for Data Selection Requires a Different Loss (and Why the Obvious Choice is Problematic)
Authors: Zilin Du, Bowen Yang, Boyang Albert Li
First: 2026-10-01T17:23:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-01T17:23:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Data selection is critical for training large language models on massive and heterogeneous corpora. Meta-learning for Training-data Selection offers a principled alternative to heuristic scoring by learning data weights from a target validation objective, but existing methods face a trade-off between fine-grained valuation and transferability to unseen data. A natural solution is to replace per-sample weights with a selection network. However, we find that directly incorporating such a network into existing MTS objectives leads to unstable optimization and poor generalization, caused by weight suppression and persistent reliance on easy-to-learn features. To address these issues, we propose Transferable Example Scoring and Selection (TESS), a scalable data-selection framework built on a Pointwise Value Matching objective (PVM). Experiments on LLM safety and targeted instruction tuning demonstrate strong transfer across datasets, from subsets to full corpora, and from smaller to larger models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可扩展的、可转移的元网络用于数据选择需要不同的损失（以及为什么明显的选择存在问题）
数据选择对于在大规模异构语料库上训练大型语言模型至关重要。用于训练数据选择的元学习提供了一种基于目标验证目标学习数据权重的原则性替代方案，但现有方法在细粒度评估和对未见数据的可转移性之间面临权衡。一个自然的解决方案是用选择网络替代每个样本的权重。然而，我们发现直接将这样的网络纳入现有的MTS目标会导致不稳定的优化和较差的泛化，原因在于权重抑制和对易学特征的持续依赖。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了可转移示例评分和选择（TESS），这是一个基于逐点价值匹配目标（PVM）构建的可扩展数据选择框架。在LLM安全性和针对性指令调优的实验中，展示了从子集到完整语料库、从小模型到大模型的强转移。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of data selection for training large language models on diverse datasets, highlighting the limitations of existing meta-learning methods that struggle with balancing fine-grained data valuation and transferability to new data. Previous approaches often lead to unstable optimization and poor generalization due to issues like weight suppression. The proposed method, Transferable Example Scoring and Selection (TESS), introduces a scalable framework that utilizes a Pointwise Value Matching objective to enhance data selection. This approach effectively mitigates the problems of existing methods and is well-motivated by the need for improved performance in data selection tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that TESS achieves strong transferability across various datasets and model sizes, supporting its effectiveness in enhancing model training outcomes.
本研究解决了在多样化数据集上训练大型语言模型时的数据选择问题，强调了现有元学习方法在平衡细粒度数据评估和对新数据的可转移性方面的局限性。以往的方法常常导致优化不稳定和泛化能力差，因为它们依赖于容易学习的特征。所提出的方法——可转移示例评分与选择（TESS），引入了一种可扩展的框架，利用逐点价值匹配目标有效地用选择网络替代每个样本的权重，从而克服了上述问题。本文贡献了一种新颖的方法论，在与大型语言模型安全性和目标指令调优相关的实验中表现出色，展示了在不同数据集和模型规模之间的有效可转移性，支持了在训练场景中改善数据选择的目标。
CHILLGuard: Towards Fine-Grained Chinese LLM Safety Guardrail with Scalable Data Construction and Model-aware Preference Alignment
Authors: Wenbo Yu, Bohua Wang, Hao Fang, Kuofeng Gao, Jingru Zeng, Xiaochen Yang, Tianyi Zhang, Xiaoxiao Ma, Jiawei Kong, Hao Wu, Bin Chen, Shu-Tao Xia, Min Zhang
Venue: EMNLP 2026
First: 2026-06-13T16:57:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-01T13:45:00+00:00
Comments: accepted by EMNLP 2026 findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Malicious content generated from large language models (LLMs) could pose severe safety risks and ethical concerns. While existing LLM safety guardrails excel in English or multilingual settings, they lack adaptation to Chinese-specific regulatory policies, cultural context, and linguistic nuances, failing to support fine-grained risk classification for diverse deployment needs. In this paper, we introduce a 5-macro, 31-micro category fine-grained risk taxonomy for Chinese scenarios, and build CHILLGuard: a dedicated Chinese LLM content safety guardrail. To address the critical scarcity of high-quality annotated Chinese safety data, we propose a scalable multi-stage data construction pipeline: we expand multi-source corpus via retrieval-augmented generation, generate implicit harmful samples through prompt engineering rewriting, and refine high-quality data via multi-model voting-based label calibration. Based on this, we build CHILLGuardTrain, a large-scale training set with 405,007 samples, and CHILLGuardTest, a rigorously curated annotated test set with 51,745 samples. We then train CHILLGuard on CHILLGuardTrain under a generator-classifier collaborative framework via Model-aware Direct Preference Optimization. Extensive experiments under multiple settings demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of CHILLGuard, e.g., a 15.92% relative improvement of F1 score over Qwen3Guard-8B-Strict on our benchmark. We release our resources at https://github.com/cswbyu/CHILLGuard.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CHILLGuard：面向细粒度中文LLM安全防护的可扩展数据构建与模型感知偏好对齐
大型语言模型（LLMs）生成的恶意内容可能带来严重的安全风险和伦理问题。现有的LLM安全防护在英语或多语言环境中表现出色，但缺乏对中国特定监管政策、文化背景和语言细微差别的适应，无法支持多样化部署需求的细粒度风险分类。本文介绍了一种针对中文场景的5个宏观、31个微观类别的细粒度风险分类法，并构建了CHILLGuard：一个专门的中文LLM内容安全防护。为了解决高质量标注中文安全数据的严重短缺，我们提出了一种可扩展的多阶段数据构建流程：通过检索增强生成扩展多源语料，通过提示工程重写生成隐性有害样本，并通过多模型投票的标签校准精炼高质量数据。在此基础上，我们构建了CHILLGuardTrain，一个包含405,007个样本的大规模训练集，以及CHILLGuardTest，一个经过严格策划的包含51,745个样本的标注测试集。然后，我们在CHILLGuardTrain上通过模型感知直接偏好优化的生成器-分类器协作框架训练CHILLGuard。多种设置下的广泛实验表明，CHILLGuard的性能达到了最先进的水平，例如，在我们的基准测试中，相较于Qwen3Guard-8B-Strict，F1分数提高了15.92%。我们在https://github.com/cswbyu/CHILLGuard发布我们的资源。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the significant safety risks and ethical concerns associated with malicious content generated by large language models (LLMs), particularly in the context of Chinese language and culture, where existing safety guardrails are inadequate. Previous methods have primarily focused on English or multilingual frameworks, failing to adapt to the specific regulatory and linguistic needs of Chinese, which has resulted in a lack of fine-grained risk classification. The proposed approach, CHILLGuard, introduces a comprehensive fine-grained risk taxonomy tailored for Chinese scenarios and employs a scalable multi-stage data construction pipeline to generate high-quality annotated safety data. This methodology includes retrieval-augmented generation, prompt engineering for harmful sample generation, and multi-model voting for label calibration, resulting in a large-scale training set of 405,007 samples and a test set of 51,745 samples. The experimental results show that CHILLGuard achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a 15.92% relative improvement in F1 score over existing models, effectively supporting its goal of enhancing content safety in Chinese LLM applications.
本研究针对大型语言模型（LLMs）生成的恶意内容所带来的重大安全风险和伦理问题，特别是在中文应用场景中，现有的安全措施显得不足。以往的方法主要集中在英语或多语言环境中，缺乏对中文特定监管政策和文化细微差别的适应性，限制了其在细粒度风险分类中的有效性。所提出的方法CHILLGuard引入了针对中文场景的全面细粒度风险分类法，并采用可扩展的多阶段数据构建流程来生成高质量的标注安全数据。该方法包括扩展多源语料库、通过提示工程生成有害样本以及通过多模型投票精炼数据。本文的贡献在于开发了CHILLGuard，该模型在大规模数据集上训练，表现出最先进的性能，在F1分数上相较于现有模型提高了15.92%，从而支持其提升中文LLMs内容安全性的目标。
In Vino Veritas and Vulnerabilities: Examining LLM Safety via Drunk Language Inducement
Authors: Anudeex Shetty, Aditya Joshi, Salil S. Kanhere
First: 2026-01-19T12:44:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-01T12:14:18+00:00
Comments: Accepted to INLG 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Humans are susceptible to undesirable behaviours and privacy leaks under the influence of alcohol. This paper investigates drunk language, i.e., text written under the influence of alcohol, as a driver for safety failures in large language models (LLMs). We investigate three mechanisms for inducing drunk language in LLMs: persona-based prompting, causal fine-tuning, and reinforcement-based post-training. When evaluated on 5 LLMs, we observe a higher susceptibility to jailbreaking on JailbreakBench (even in the presence of defences) and privacy leaks on ConfAIde, where both benchmarks are in English, as compared to the base LLMs as well as previously reported approaches. Via a robust combination of manual evaluation and LLM-based evaluators and analysis of error categories, our findings highlight a correspondence between human-intoxicated behaviour, and anthropomorphism in LLMs induced with drunk language. The simplicity and efficiency of our drunk language inducement approaches position them as potential counters for LLM safety tuning, highlighting significant risks to LLM safety.
中文标题/摘要
标题：酒中真理与脆弱性：通过醉酒语言诱导考察大型语言模型的安全性
人类在酒精影响下容易出现不良行为和隐私泄露。本文研究醉酒语言，即在酒精影响下撰写的文本，作为大型语言模型（LLMs）安全失败的驱动因素。我们探讨了三种在LLMs中诱导醉酒语言的机制：基于角色的提示、因果微调和基于强化的后训练。在对5个LLMs进行评估时，我们观察到在JailbreakBench上（即使在防御存在的情况下）对越狱的更高易感性，以及在ConfAIde上的隐私泄露，这两个基准均为英语，相较于基础LLMs和之前报告的方法。通过手动评估与基于LLM的评估者的稳健组合以及错误类别的分析，我们的发现突显了人类醉酒行为与通过醉酒语言诱导的LLMs中的拟人化之间的对应关系。我们诱导醉酒语言的方法简单高效，使其成为LLM安全调优的潜在对策，突显了对LLM安全的重大风险。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the vulnerabilities of large language models (LLMs) to safety failures and privacy leaks, drawing a parallel between human behavior under alcohol influence and LLM responses. Previous methods for evaluating LLM safety have not effectively captured these risks, particularly in terms of jailbreaking and privacy issues. The proposed approach introduces three mechanisms for inducing 'drunk language' in LLMs—persona-based prompting, causal fine-tuning, and reinforcement-based post-training—offering a novel perspective on LLM safety tuning. The methodology includes evaluations on five LLMs using JailbreakBench and ConfAIde benchmarks, revealing increased susceptibility to safety failures compared to base models and prior methods. The findings demonstrate that the proposed methods effectively highlight significant risks to LLM safety, supporting the need for improved safety measures in LLM development.
本文探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）的安全漏洞，研究了醉酒语言的概念，即在酒精影响下生成的文本。以往评估LLM安全性的方法未能有效捕捉人类不良行为和隐私泄露的细微差别，导致对LLM脆弱性的理解不足。提出的方法引入了三种诱导LLM醉酒语言的机制：基于角色的提示、因果微调和基于强化的后训练，这些方法有效模拟了人类醉酒的影响。本文的贡献在于证明LLMs在接触醉酒语言时对越狱和隐私泄露的敏感性增加，这在JailbreakBench和ConfAIde的评估中得到了证实。该方法结合了手动评估和基于LLM的评估器来分析错误类别，揭示了LLM安全性面临的重大风险，并表明所提出的方法可以作为增强LLM安全调优的有效工具。
False Floors: LLM Safety Routing Evaluations Break Under Distribution Shift
Authors: Amit Singh Bhatti, Vishal Vaddina
First: 2026-10-01T12:09:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-01T12:09:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safety routers send each request to one of several models and are judged against the best single model. A major routing benchmark picks that comparator on the evaluation data. In the benchmark's own setting this is harmless, but under distribution shift it is not. On HELM Safety the selection cost is 0.003-0.030 of harm under random splits and 0.045-0.113 under held-out categories, comparable to the whole deficit attributed to routing, with its direction holding under either published judge alone. It rises seven- to ninefold on AgentDojo when suites are held out. Across seven safety corpora chosen by rules fixed in advance, three meet a registered interval test and four beat a later permutation null, and three of the four interval misses are corpora where some models have zero observed harm. Prior work proves the direction of this bias. We size it on harm and accuracy, show that it is larger under the held-out splits we measure, and bound it by optimism plus a shift-dependent regret. Scored honestly under shift, routing buys little on these benchmarks. In most pool cells the nested router serves the honest baseline's model, and on the nearly saturated AgentDojo corpus a perfect pre-dispatch router is worth at most two points of harm. We also find a model's expressed recognition of a late injection steerable. On held-out reruns an attacker who knows which model it faces lowers GPT-5.4's judged recognition by 19.6 points, confirmed by an independent label. In an offline counterfactual composition into a controller, the same attack raises or lowers estimated harm depending on the fallback model. Safety routing should be evaluated under shift, against a baseline chosen without the test labels, and recognition-based defences should be scored on harm against an attacker who chooses what the model sees.
中文标题/摘要
标题：虚假底层：LLM安全路由评估在分布变化下失效
安全路由器将每个请求发送到多个模型中的一个，并与最佳单一模型进行比较。一个主要的路由基准在评估数据中选择该比较器。在基准自身的设置中这是无害的，但在分布变化下则不是。在HELM安全中，选择成本在随机拆分下为0.003-0.030的损害，在保留类别下为0.045-0.113，与路由所归因的整体缺口相当，其方向在单独发布的评判者下保持不变。当套件被保留时，在AgentDojo上增加了七到九倍。在七个由预先固定规则选择的安全语料库中，三个满足注册区间测试，四个超过后来的置换零，而四个区间缺失中的三个是一些模型观察到的损害为零的语料库。先前的工作证明了这种偏差的方向。我们在损害和准确性上对其进行了量化，显示在我们测量的保留拆分下更大，并通过乐观加上依赖于变化的遗憾进行界定。在变化下诚实评分，路由在这些基准上几乎没有收益。在大多数池单元中，嵌套路由器服务于诚实基线的模型，而在几乎饱和的AgentDojo语料库上，一个完美的预调度路由器最多值两个损害点。我们还发现模型对晚期注入的表达识别是可引导的。在保留重跑中，知道面对哪个模型的攻击者将GPT-5.4的评判识别降低了19.6点，得到了独立标签的确认。在离线反事实组成到控制器中，同样的攻击根据后备模型的不同提高或降低估计的损害。安全路由应在变化下进行评估，对比未使用测试标签选择的基线，基于识别的防御应在面对选择模型所见内容的攻击者时按损害进行评分。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the limitations of safety routers that evaluate requests by comparing them to the best-performing model, particularly under distribution shifts where existing benchmarks fail to accurately assess performance. Previous methods inadequately account for the harm caused by routing decisions, especially when evaluation data does not represent real-world scenarios. The proposed approach emphasizes the need for evaluating safety routing under distribution shifts and suggests that current benchmarks may underestimate the harm caused by routing. The methodology involves analyzing safety performance across multiple corpora and measuring harm and accuracy under various conditions. The findings reveal that routing provides minimal benefits in terms of harm reduction, with significant increases in harm observed under held-out splits, indicating that safety routing evaluations must be reformed to ensure reliability and accuracy in real-world applications.
本研究探讨了现有安全路由器的局限性，这些路由器通过将请求与单一最佳模型进行比较来进行评估，尤其是在分布变化的情况下，这种方法变得问题重重。以往的方法未能充分考虑这种变化引入的偏差，导致模型安全性的评估存在误导。所提出的方法强调在分布变化下评估安全路由的必要性，并表明路由性能通常被高估，因为它经常回归到基线模型的性能。本文的贡献在于量化安全路由评估中的偏差，并证明路由造成的危害在某些情况下可能显著，尤其是在保留的场景中。该方法论涉及对多个安全语料库的性能分析，并显示在这些条件下路由提供的好处微乎其微，具体指标表明在许多情况下未能达到性能目标。
TensorCommitments: A Lightweight Verifiable Inference for Language Models
Authors: Oguzhan Baser, Elahe Sadeghi, Eric Wang, Nico Vergauwen, Sam Kazemian, Hong Kang, Sandeep P. Chinchali, Sriram Vishwanath
First: 2026-02-13T05:23:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-01T09:29:49+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Most large language models (LLMs) run on external clouds: users send a prompt, pay for inference, and must trust that the remote GPU executes the LLM without any adversarial tampering. We critically ask how to achieve verifiable LLM inference, where a prover (the service) must convince a verifier (the client) that an inference was run correctly without rerunning the LLM. Existing cryptographic works are too slow at the LLM scale, while non-cryptographic ones require a strong verifier GPU. We propose TensorCommitments (TCs), a tensor-native proof-of-inference scheme. TC binds the LLM inference to a commitment, an irreversible tag that breaks under tampering, organized in our multivariate Terkle Trees. For LLaMA2, TC adds only 0.97% prover and 0.12% verifier time over inference while improving robustness to tailored LLM attacks by up to 48% over the best prior work requiring a verifier GPU.
中文标题/摘要
标题：张量承诺：一种轻量级可验证的语言模型推理
大多数大型语言模型（LLMs）运行在外部云上：用户发送提示，支付推理费用，并必须信任远程GPU在没有任何对抗性篡改的情况下执行LLM。我们批判性地询问如何实现可验证的LLM推理，其中证明者（服务）必须说服验证者（客户端）在不重新运行LLM的情况下推理是正确的。现有的密码学工作在LLM规模上速度太慢，而非密码学的方案则需要强大的验证者GPU。我们提出了张量承诺（TC），一种张量原生的推理证明方案。TC将LLM推理绑定到承诺上，这是一个在篡改下会破坏的不可逆标签，组织在我们的多变量特克尔树中。对于LLaMA2，TC在推理上仅增加0.97%的证明者时间和0.12%的验证者时间，同时将针对定制LLM攻击的鲁棒性提高了多达48%，相比于需要验证者GPU的最佳先前工作。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the challenge of achieving verifiable inference in large language models (LLMs) that operate on external clouds, where users must trust that their prompts are processed correctly without tampering. Previous methods, including cryptographic approaches, are often too slow for LLM applications, while non-cryptographic methods necessitate powerful verifier GPUs, limiting their practicality. The proposed TensorCommitments (TCs) scheme introduces a tensor-native proof-of-inference mechanism that binds LLM inference to a commitment, utilizing multivariate Terkle Trees to ensure integrity without the need for re-execution. This approach is well-motivated as it enhances the robustness of LLMs against tailored attacks while maintaining minimal overhead, adding only 0.97% to prover time and 0.12% to verifier time for the LLaMA2 model, and achieving a 48% improvement in robustness over existing methods requiring a verifier GPU.
本研究解决了在外部云服务上运行的大型语言模型（LLM）中确保可验证推理的挑战，用户必须信任其提示在没有篡改的情况下被正确处理。以往的方法，包括加密方法，速度对于LLM规模来说过于缓慢，而非加密方法则需要强大的验证者GPU，限制了其实用性。提出的张量承诺（TC）方案提供了一种张量原生的推理证明，将LLM推理绑定到承诺上，利用多变量Terkle树增强对篡改的安全性。该方法动机充分，显著提高了对定制LLM攻击的鲁棒性，相较于现有方法提高了多达48%的防护能力，同时在LLaMA2模型上推理时仅增加了0.97%的证明者和0.12%的验证者时间，从而有效支持可验证推理的目标。
Easier Said Than Done: Unpacking Intent-Behavior Gap in Jailbreaking LLM-Based Robots
Authors: Xuancun Lu, Zhengxian Huang, Xinfeng Li, Chi Zhang, Xiaoyu Ji, Wenyuan Xu
First: 2024-12-21T13:58:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-10-01T01:33:58+00:00
Comments: Accepted by NDSS 2027
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
LLM-based robots use Large Language Models (LLMs) as planners to translate natural language instructions into policies such as grasp(), move_to(), and open_gripper(). Jailbreak attacks on these robots extend the threat from generating malicious content to executing harmful behaviors. However, we find that existing jailbreak attempts against LLM-based robots that produce malicious-looking policies (intent jailbreaks) often fail to induce harmful physical actions by robots (behavior jailbreaks), due to robot-specific constraints, such as logical errors and hallucinated control APIs. In this paper, we demystify the intent-behavior gap and investigate its root causes to inform effective defenses. Our measurement study finds that current LLM jailbreak methods overlook robot-specific syntax constraints (e.g., executable control APIs) and physical feasibility (e.g., ordering of policies and hardware/kinematic constraints). To bridge the gap, we introduce POEF (POlicy EFfective Jailbreak), an automated red-teaming framework that takes into account the robot-specific constraints during both the optimization and evaluation processes. Specifically, POEF employs the hidden-layer gradients from an unaligned LLM to guide the jailbreak prompt optimization and uses a multi-agent evaluator to assess the feasibility of the generated policies. Experiments on commercial robots, including the Unitree G1, the Franka robotic arm, and simulators, show that POEF achieves an 80% behavior jailbreak success rate and transfers across various LLMs. In addition, we propose two defense strategies that mitigate the behavior jailbreak risks. Our findings indicate an urgent need for stronger countermeasures before LLM-based robots are deployed at scale. The homepage is available at https://zjushine.github.io/poef.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：说起来容易做起来难：揭示越狱 LLM 基于机器人的意图-行为差距
基于 LLM 的机器人使用大型语言模型 (LLM) 作为规划者，将自然语言指令转换为策略，如 grasp()、move_to() 和 open_gripper()。对这些机器人的越狱攻击将威胁从生成恶意内容扩展到执行有害行为。然而，我们发现，现有针对 LLM 基于机器人的越狱尝试（意图越狱）往往未能引发机器人（行为越狱）的有害物理动作，原因在于机器人特有的约束，如逻辑错误和虚构的控制 API。本文揭示了意图-行为差距，并调查其根本原因，以便提供有效的防御。我们的测量研究发现，当前的 LLM 越狱方法忽视了机器人特有的语法约束（例如，可执行的控制 API）和物理可行性（例如，策略的顺序和硬件/运动学约束）。为了解决这一差距，我们引入了 POEF（策略有效越狱），这是一个自动化的红队框架，在优化和评估过程中考虑机器人特有的约束。具体而言，POEF 利用未对齐 LLM 的隐藏层梯度来指导越狱提示的优化，并使用多智能体评估器来评估生成策略的可行性。在包括 Unitree G1、Franka 机械臂和模拟器在内的商业机器人上的实验表明，POEF 实现了 80% 的行为越狱成功率，并在各种 LLM 之间转移。此外，我们提出了两种防御策略，以减轻行为越狱风险。我们的研究结果表明，在大规模部署基于 LLM 的机器人之前，迫切需要更强的对策。主页可访问 https://zjushine.github.io/poef.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the vulnerabilities of LLM-based robots to jailbreak attacks, which can lead to harmful behaviors despite existing methods primarily focusing on generating malicious-looking policies. Previous approaches have failed to consider robot-specific constraints, such as executable control APIs and physical feasibility, resulting in a disconnect between intent and actual behavior. This paper introduces POEF (Policy Effective Jailbreak), an automated red-teaming framework that incorporates these constraints during optimization and evaluation, thereby effectively bridging the intent-behavior gap. The methodology utilizes hidden-layer gradients from an unaligned LLM for prompt optimization and employs a multi-agent evaluator to assess policy feasibility. Experiments conducted on various commercial robots demonstrate an 80% success rate in behavior jailbreaks, highlighting the effectiveness of the proposed method and underscoring the necessity for enhanced defenses before widespread deployment of LLM-based robots.
本研究关注LLM驱动的机器人在越狱攻击中的脆弱性，这可能导致有害行为，尽管现有方法主要集中在生成恶意内容上。以往的方法往往忽视了机器人特定的约束条件，如逻辑错误和控制API的可行性。提出的方法POEF（政策有效越狱）通过将这些约束纳入优化和评估过程来有所不同，利用未对齐LLM的隐藏层梯度和多智能体评估器来增强越狱提示的有效性。本文的贡献显著，POEF在各种商业机器人和模拟器上实现了80%的行为越狱成功率，突显了在LLM驱动的机器人广泛部署之前需要改进防御措施的紧迫性。
Pretext: Defeating Malicious Skill Detection Frameworks for AI Agents
Authors: Tobias Kaisar, Aritra Dhar
Venue: NeurIPS 2026
First: 2026-09-30T12:26:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-30T12:26:33+00:00
Comments: Accepted in AIWild@NeurIPS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Skills extend an agent's capabilities by injecting instructions and information into the context, and are widely used by agents such as OpenClaw and Claude Code. Prior work shows third-party marketplaces host malicious skills that give attackers direct influence over the victim's agent. The emerging defense scans skills before installation, pairing deterministic static checks with an LLM-based semantic judge, as in NVIDIA's SkillSpector. We show that such defenses fall to an attacker who knows the detector. Our white-box LLM attacker, Pretext, iteratively crafts skills that evade detection while still delivering the payload and performing the benign task: moving the payload from code into natural language leaves static analysis inert, while framing it as the skill's legitimate purpose and splitting instructions across files keeps the LLM stage below its blocking threshold. Across three open-source models, Pretext achieves up to 97\% and 77\% against a frozen detector and a co-adaptive one, respectively, revealing major gaps in current skill scanners.
中文标题/摘要
标题：前提：击败恶意技能检测框架的AI代理
技能通过将指令和信息注入上下文来扩展代理的能力，广泛应用于OpenClaw和Claude Code等代理。先前的研究表明，第三方市场托管恶意技能，使攻击者对受害者的代理产生直接影响。新兴的防御措施在安装前扫描技能，将确定性静态检查与基于LLM的语义判断相结合，如NVIDIA的SkillSpector。我们表明，这种防御措施会被了解检测器的攻击者攻破。我们的白盒LLM攻击者Pretext迭代地构造能够规避检测的技能，同时仍然传递有效载荷并执行良性任务：将有效载荷从代码转移到自然语言使静态分析失效，而将其框定为技能的合法目的并将指令分散到多个文件中则使LLM阶段保持在其阻断阈值以下。在三个开源模型中，Pretext在冻结检测器和共适应检测器上分别达到了97%和77%的成功率，揭示了当前技能扫描器的重大缺陷。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the vulnerability of AI agents to malicious skills that can be injected through third-party marketplaces, which can compromise the agent's functionality. Previous methods, such as NVIDIA's SkillSpector, utilize static checks and LLM-based semantic judges to detect these malicious skills; however, they are ineffective against attackers who understand the detection mechanisms. The proposed approach, Pretext, innovatively crafts skills that evade detection while still achieving their intended purpose, thus overcoming the limitations of existing methods. This paper contributes by demonstrating the effectiveness of Pretext in bypassing current defenses, achieving detection evasion rates of up to 97% against a static detector and 77% against a co-adaptive one across three open-source models, highlighting significant weaknesses in existing skill scanning technologies.
本研究关注AI代理面临的恶意技能的脆弱性，这些技能可以通过第三方市场注入，强调了现有防御措施（如NVIDIA的SkillSpector）的不足，该措施结合了静态检查和基于LLM的语义分析。以往的方法在攻击者了解检测机制时失效，使他们能够设计逃避检测的技能，同时执行有害任务。提出的方法Pretext利用白盒LLM攻击者迭代设计技能，通过模糊恶意意图和将指令分散到多个文件中来绕过这些防御。该方法显示出显著的有效性，在静态检测器面前实现了高达97%的逃避检测率，在自适应检测器面前实现了77%的逃避检测率，从而揭示了当前技能扫描技术的关键弱点，并强调了AI系统中改进安全措施的必要性。
$D^2$-Monitor: Dynamic Safety Monitoring for Diffusion LLMs via Hesitation Signals
Authors: Aoxi Liu, Yupeng Chen, James Oldfield, Guanzhe Hong, Junchi Yu, Baoyuan Wu, Philip Torr, Adel Bibi
First: 2026-05-25T14:22:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-30T09:49:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite the emergence of diffusion large language models (D-LLMs) as an alternative to autoregressive large language models (AR-LLMs), safety monitoring for D-LLMs remains largely unexplored. Unlike AR-LLMs, D-LLMs generate text through a multi-step denoising process, exposing intermediate hidden representations that may contain safety-relevant information unavailable in standard single-step monitoring setups. Motivated by the suitability of lightweight probes for always-on monitoring, we analyze which trajectory-level signals best indicate when such probes are likely to struggle. We find that the most informative signal is safety hesitation: intermediate hidden states repeatedly falling within a small margin of the probe's decision boundary. The number of such hesitation steps in D-LLM's trajectory predicts probe failure effectively, providing a proxy for sample difficulty. Building on this analysis, we propose $D^2$-Monitor, in which a lightweight probe runs on every sample, producing a base prediction and a hesitation estimate that decides whether to escalate the sample to a more expressive probe. That probe is trained on trajectories with at least one hesitation step, and reads only the minimal span of those steps. Evaluated on 3 datasets (WildGuardMix, ToxicChat, OpenAI-Moderation) across 4 D-LLMs, $D^2$-Monitor achieves state-of-the-art performance with a compact parameter footprint ($\leq$ 0.93M parameters), and exhibits the best trade-off between effectiveness and efficiency relative to 8 baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：$D^2$-监测器：通过犹豫信号对扩散大语言模型进行动态安全监测
尽管扩散大语言模型（D-LLMs）作为自回归大语言模型（AR-LLMs）的替代方案已出现，但对D-LLMs的安全监测仍然 largely 未被探索。与AR-LLMs不同，D-LLMs通过多步骤去噪过程生成文本，暴露出可能包含安全相关信息的中间隐藏表示，而这些信息在标准单步监测设置中不可用。基于轻量级探针适合于始终在线监测的动机，我们分析了哪些轨迹级信号最能指示这些探针可能遇到困难。我们发现，最具信息量的信号是安全犹豫：中间隐藏状态反复落在探针决策边界的小范围内。D-LLM轨迹中的这种犹豫步骤数量有效预测探针失败，提供了样本难度的代理。基于此分析，我们提出了$D^2$-监测器，其中轻量级探针在每个样本上运行，产生基本预测和犹豫估计，以决定是否将样本升级到更具表现力的探针。该探针在至少有一个犹豫步骤的轨迹上进行训练，并仅读取这些步骤的最小范围。在3个数据集（WildGuardMix、ToxicChat、OpenAI-Moderation）上评估4个D-LLMs，$D^2$-监测器以紧凑的参数占用（≤ 0.93M参数）实现了最先进的性能，并在相对于8个基线的有效性和效率之间展现了最佳的权衡。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the gap in safety monitoring for diffusion large language models (D-LLMs), which utilize a multi-step denoising process, unlike autoregressive models (AR-LLMs) that have been more extensively studied. Previous methods have not effectively utilized the intermediate hidden representations of D-LLMs, leading to inadequate safety assessments. The proposed $D^2$-Monitor introduces a lightweight probe that continuously monitors samples and uses safety hesitation signals—instances where hidden states hover near the decision boundary—to predict probe failures and sample difficulty. This method is well-motivated as it leverages the unique characteristics of D-LLMs for improved monitoring. The contribution of the paper lies in its innovative approach to safety monitoring, achieving state-of-the-art performance on three datasets (WildGuardMix, ToxicChat, OpenAI-Moderation) with a compact model size of less than 0.93 million parameters, demonstrating a superior balance between effectiveness and efficiency compared to eight existing baselines.
本研究针对扩散大型语言模型（D-LLMs）安全监测的空白进行探讨，这一领域相较于自回归大型语言模型（AR-LLMs）尚未得到充分研究。以往的方法主要集中在单步监测上，无法捕捉D-LLMs的多步去噪过程，导致安全评估不足。所提出的方法$D^2$-Monitor利用轻量级探针持续监测D-LLMs，通过分析轨迹级信号，特别是安全犹豫信号，来指示潜在的探针失败。这一方法具有良好的动机，因为它有效预测样本难度并提高监测准确性。本文的贡献在于开发了$D^2$-Monitor，该方法在三个数据集（WildGuardMix、ToxicChat、OpenAI-Moderation）上对四个D-LLMs实现了最先进的性能，展现出相较于八个基线方法在有效性和效率之间的优越平衡。
Faithful Dual-constrained Erasure for Robust LLM Safety Alignment
Authors: Jiaqing Li, Shide Zhou, Zhibo Zhang, Yuxi Li, Tianlong Yu, Kailong Wang
First: 2026-09-30T08:29:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-30T08:29:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine unlearning has emerged as a crucial mechanism for removing hazardous knowledge and enforcing safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs). However, recent studies reveal a persistent security risk: unlearned models remain highly vulnerable to retraining attacks, where suppressed malicious behaviors rapidly resurface after benign fine-tuning. In this work, we investigate the optimization dynamics of unlearning and identify that this vulnerability stems from shallow alignment. Rather than effectively erasing target knowledge, models often exploit a shortcut by activating previously dormant parameters to act as spurious suppressors, forming a fragile inhibitory shell over intact malicious representations. To address this issue and enforce authentic memory deletion, we propose FDCU, a novel dual-constrained subspace projection framework. FDCU restricts parameter updates through a highly scalable, element-wise dual-masking rule: it preserves general knowledge manifolds via Fisher Information and strictly prohibits the abnormal activation of spurious suppressors via the Principle of Minimal Functional Intervention (PMFI). By reliably blocking the model's ability to superficially hide knowledge, FDCU promotes the authentic dismantling of target representations. Extensive experiments across specific knowledge erasure and safe output control tasks demonstrate that FDCU achieves state-of-the-art robustness against retraining attacks while maintaining near-lossless general utility, ensuring durable safety for LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可靠的双约束消除机制用于增强大型语言模型的安全对齐
机器遗忘已成为去除危险知识和强化大型语言模型（LLMs）安全对齐的重要机制。然而，最近的研究揭示了一个持续的安全风险：被遗忘的模型仍然高度易受再训练攻击的影响，抑制的恶意行为在良性微调后迅速复发。在本研究中，我们探讨了遗忘的优化动态，并确定这种脆弱性源于浅层对齐。模型往往通过激活先前休眠的参数作为虚假抑制器来利用捷径，而不是有效地消除目标知识，从而在完整的恶意表示上形成脆弱的抑制壳。为了解决这个问题并强制执行真实的记忆删除，我们提出了FDCU，一种新颖的双约束子空间投影框架。FDCU通过高度可扩展的逐元素双掩蔽规则限制参数更新：它通过费舍尔信息保留一般知识流形，并严格禁止通过最小功能干预原则（PMFI）异常激活虚假抑制器。通过可靠地阻止模型表面隐藏知识的能力，FDCU促进了目标表示的真实拆解。在特定知识消除和安全输出控制任务中的广泛实验表明，FDCU在抵御再训练攻击方面实现了最先进的鲁棒性，同时保持近乎无损的一般效用，确保了LLMs的持久安全。

The research addresses the critical issue of machine unlearning in Large Language Models (LLMs), particularly the vulnerability of unlearned models to retraining attacks that can reactivate suppressed malicious behaviors. Previous methods inadequately handled this problem, often allowing models to exploit dormant parameters instead of effectively erasing harmful knowledge. The proposed approach, FDCU, introduces a dual-constrained subspace projection framework that utilizes a scalable dual-masking rule to ensure genuine memory deletion while preserving essential knowledge. This method significantly enhances robustness against retraining attacks and maintains high general utility, as demonstrated through extensive experiments on knowledge erasure and safe output control tasks, achieving state-of-the-art performance in ensuring LLM safety alignment.

How to Tame a Multi-Headed Hydra? Adaptive Multi-Category Safety Steering for Large Language Models
Authors: Chenxi Wang, Ruiyang Huang, Li Huang, Yifan Wu
First: 2026-09-28T07:53:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-29T07:14:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As large language models (LLMs) become increasingly widespread, preventing unsafe responses to harmful prompts is essential for their safe deployment. Activation steering offers an approach to improving LLM safety by modifying internal activations during inference without updating model parameters. However, a single prompt can involve multiple harm categories, and steering toward safety in one category may leave harmful content from another unaddressed. Despite advances in adaptive steering, existing methods do not explicitly coordinate steering direction and strength when multiple harm categories co-occur within a single prompt. To address this problem, we propose CAM-Steer, a Category-Adaptive Multi-category Safety Steering framework. Specifically, it estimates the risk associated with each harm category by comparing the current hidden state with safe and unsafe prototypes. The estimated risks are then used to combine the safety directions for different harm categories into a single steering direction and to determine the strength of the intervention. Finally, it rotates the hidden state along the composed steering direction, with the rotation angle determined by the estimated risks, while preserving the hidden-state norm. Experiments across three LLM backbones and seven harm categories show that CAM-Steer outperforms the evaluated baselines in average defense success rate, including when categories co-occur. Further analyses support its component designs and informative risk scores, with negligible inference overhead.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如何驯服多头水蛇？大型语言模型的自适应多类别安全引导
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越普及，防止对有害提示的安全响应对于其安全部署至关重要。激活引导提供了一种通过在推理过程中修改内部激活而不更新模型参数来提高LLM安全性的方法。然而，单个提示可能涉及多个危害类别，而在一个类别中引导安全可能会忽视另一个类别中的有害内容。尽管自适应引导有所进展，但现有方法在多个危害类别同时出现时并未明确协调引导方向和强度。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了CAM-Steer，一个类别自适应多类别安全引导框架。具体而言，它通过将当前隐藏状态与安全和不安全原型进行比较来估计与每个危害类别相关的风险。然后，利用估计的风险将不同危害类别的安全方向组合成一个单一的引导方向，并确定干预的强度。最后，它沿着组合的引导方向旋转隐藏状态，旋转角度由估计的风险决定，同时保持隐藏状态的范数。在三个LLM骨干和七个危害类别的实验中，CAM-Steer在平均防御成功率上优于评估的基线，包括类别共现时。进一步分析支持其组件设计和信息丰富的风险评分，推理开销微乎其微。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the critical issue of ensuring safety in large language models (LLMs) by preventing unsafe responses to harmful prompts, a challenge exacerbated by the presence of multiple harm categories in a single prompt. Previous methods, while utilizing activation steering to enhance safety, often fail to coordinate the steering direction and strength effectively when multiple harm categories are present, leading to incomplete safety measures. The proposed CAM-Steer framework introduces a Category-Adaptive Multi-category Safety Steering approach that estimates risks for each harm category by comparing hidden states with safe and unsafe prototypes, allowing for a coordinated steering direction and intervention strength. This method is well-motivated as it directly addresses the limitations of existing adaptive steering techniques. Experimental results demonstrate that CAM-Steer significantly improves the average defense success rate across three LLM backbones and seven harm categories, effectively managing co-occurring risks while maintaining low inference overhead.
本研究解决了大型语言模型（LLMs）在处理有害提示时确保安全性的问题，随着这些模型的普及，这一挑战变得尤为重要。以往的方法，如激活引导，虽然在推理过程中修改内部激活，但未能有效管理可能在单个提示中共存的多个危害类别，导致安全措施不完整。提出的CAM-Steer框架通过估计每个危害类别的风险，并将这些风险整合以创建统一的引导方向和干预强度，从而改善了这些方法，确保更全面的安全响应。本文贡献了一种新的自适应多类别安全引导方法，通过在三个LLM骨干和七个危害类别上的实验，表明CAM-Steer显著提高了平均防御成功率，即使在多个有害类别共存的情况下，且保持了较低的推理开销。
When Do Model Internals Help? Exploring the Role of Representation Engineering in LLM Safety
Authors: Tianyi Guan, Jianhui Chen, Liangming Pan
First: 2026-09-28T09:50:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-28T09:50:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable AI safeguards require both control mechanisms that reduce unsafe behavior and monitoring mechanisms that detect safety risks during model interactions. Established behavioral safeguards include alignment methods that optimize model outputs and text monitors that assess interaction text. Representation engineering instead reads or modifies internal model states, but the relative strengths of these approaches remain unclear because they are often evaluated under different settings. We present a matched evaluation across two tracks. For safety control, we compare DPO, a behavioral alignment method, with three representation steering methods across robustness, practicality, and granularity. DPO provides the strongest overall control and generally improves with increasing training data, although its safety can degrade after subsequent benign fine-tuning. Representation steering remains competitive primarily in low-data settings, particularly with high-quality contrastive data. For safety monitoring, we compare representation probes with fine-tuned and open-weight text monitors across full-response detection, early detection, and computational cost. Specialized text monitors achieve the strongest overall detection accuracy, while representation probes remain competitive at substantially lower marginal cost. Finally, monitor-guided interventions recover much of the safety lost by DPO after benign fine-tuning, with little additional over-refusal. Overall, representation engineering does not generally replace behavioral safeguards, but offers practical advantages under specific conditions and can provide complementary safety benefits.
中文标题/摘要
标题：模型内部何时有帮助？探索表示工程在大型语言模型安全性中的作用
可靠的人工智能安全措施需要控制机制以减少不安全行为，以及监测机制以在模型交互中检测安全风险。既定的行为安全措施包括优化模型输出的对齐方法和评估交互文本的文本监测器。表示工程则读取或修改内部模型状态，但这些方法的相对优势仍不清楚，因为它们通常在不同的设置下进行评估。我们在两个轨道上进行了匹配评估。对于安全控制，我们比较了行为对齐方法DPO与三种表示引导方法在鲁棒性、实用性和粒度方面的表现。DPO提供了最强的整体控制，并且通常随着训练数据的增加而改善，尽管在后续的良性微调后，其安全性可能会下降。表示引导在低数据设置中仍具有竞争力，特别是在高质量对比数据的情况下。对于安全监测，我们比较了表示探针与微调和开放权重的文本监测器在完整响应检测、早期检测和计算成本方面的表现。专业的文本监测器实现了最强的整体检测准确性，而表示探针在边际成本显著较低的情况下仍具有竞争力。最后，监测引导的干预恢复了DPO在良性微调后失去的许多安全性，几乎没有额外的拒绝。总体而言，表示工程通常并不取代行为安全措施，但在特定条件下提供了实用优势，并可以提供互补的安全益处。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the need for reliable AI safeguards that can both control unsafe behavior and monitor safety risks during model interactions. Previous methods primarily focused on behavioral alignment and text monitoring, which have limitations in robustness and adaptability, particularly in low-data scenarios. The proposed approach introduces representation engineering as a complementary strategy, evaluating its effectiveness alongside established methods in a matched evaluation framework. This study contributes by demonstrating that while behavioral alignment methods like DPO generally provide stronger control, representation steering can be advantageous in low-data contexts and offers cost-effective monitoring solutions. The methodology involves comparing DPO with representation steering methods for safety control and assessing representation probes against text monitors for safety monitoring, revealing that specialized text monitors yield the highest detection accuracy while representation probes maintain competitive performance at lower costs, ultimately enhancing model safety through monitor-guided interventions.
本研究关注于可靠的人工智能安全措施，以减轻大型语言模型（LLMs）的不安全行为并监测安全风险。以往的方法主要集中在行为对齐和文本监测上，由于评估设置不同，比较效果的清晰度往往不足。本文提出对表示工程与现有方法进行匹配评估，强调尽管行为对齐方法DPO通常提供更强的控制，但在低数据场景下，表示引导可能具有优势。该研究通过系统比较这些方法在安全控制和监测任务中的表现，揭示了专门的文本监测器提供最佳检测准确性，而表示探针则是成本效益高的替代方案。最终，研究结果表明，表示工程在特定情况下补充而非替代现有的安全措施。
ArGuard Shared Task: Harmful Content Detection in Arabic Memes and LLM Prompts
Authors: Firoj Alam, Md. Rafiul Biswas, Mohamed Bayan Kmainasi, Ali Ezzat Shahroor, Hamdy Mubarak, George Mikros, Abul Hasnat, Wajdi Zaghouani
First: 2026-09-24T10:22:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-25T14:21:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
ArGuard is a shared task on harmful content detection in Arabic memes and LLM prompts. It includes two tracks: Track A focuses on multimodal hate detection in Arabic memes, while Track B addresses harmful prompt detection for Arabic LLM safety evaluation. In total, 58 teams registered, 35 participated in the final evaluation, and 27 submitted system-description papers. Participating teams explored models such as AraBERT, Jais, and Qwen3-VL. The best systems achieved macro-F1 scores of 0.823 on A1, 0.419 on A2, 0.984 on B1, and 0.790 on B2. Fine-grained meme classification in A2 was the most challenging setting, partly due to sparse labels and train-test distribution shifts.

标题：ArGuard共享任务：阿拉伯语表情包和LLM提示中的有害内容检测

The research focuses on the detection of harmful content in Arabic memes and LLM prompts, addressing a significant gap in the safety evaluation of Arabic language models. How Many Iterations to Jailbreak? Dynamic Budget Allocation for Multi-Turn LLM Evaluation
Authors: Shai Feldman, Yaniv Romano
First: 2026-05-07T17:25:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-24T19:53:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evaluating and predicting the performance of large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversational settings is critical yet computationally expensive; key events---e.g., jailbreaks or successful task completion by an agent---often emerge only after repeated interactions. These events might be rare and, under any feasible computational budget, remain unobserved. Recent conformal survival frameworks construct reliable lower predictive bounds (LPBs) on the number of iterations to trigger the event of interest, but rely on static budget allocation that prohibits adaptivity in multi-turn setups. To address this, we introduce Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization (DAPRO), a theoretically valid dynamic budget allocation framework for bounding the time-to-event in multi-turn LLM interactions. We prove that DAPRO satisfies the expected budget constraint and provides distribution-free, finite-sample coverage guarantees without requiring the conditional independence between censoring and event times assumed by prior conformal survival approaches. A key theoretical contribution is a novel coverage bound that scales with the square root of the mean censoring weight over only a subset of the samples rather than the worst-case weight, yielding tighter guarantees than prior work. Furthermore, DAPRO can be employed to obtain unbiased estimates of population-level evaluation metrics, such as the jailbreak rate, under limited computing resources. Comprehensive experiments across agentic task success, LLM jailbreaks, toxic content generation, and RAG hallucinations demonstrate that DAPRO achieves coverage closer to the nominal level with lower variance than static baselines, while not exceeding the budget constraint in expectation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：越狱需要多少次迭代？多轮LLM评估的动态预算分配
在多轮对话环境中评估和预测大型语言模型（LLM）的性能至关重要，但计算成本高；关键事件——例如越狱或代理成功完成任务——通常仅在反复交互后出现。这些事件可能很少，并且在任何可行的计算预算下，仍然未被观察到。最近的保形生存框架构建了触发感兴趣事件所需迭代次数的可靠下限预测界（LPB），但依赖于静态预算分配，限制了多轮设置中的适应性。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了通过投影优化的动态分配（DAPRO），这是一个理论上有效的动态预算分配框架，用于界定多轮LLM交互中的事件发生时间。我们证明了DAPRO满足预期预算约束，并提供无分布、有限样本覆盖保证，而不需要先前保形生存方法假设的审查和事件时间之间的条件独立性。一个关键的理论贡献是一个新的覆盖界限，它与仅在样本子集上计算的平均审查权重的平方根成比例，而不是最坏情况下的权重，从而提供比先前工作更紧的保证。此外，DAPRO可以在有限计算资源下获得人口级评估指标的无偏估计，例如越狱率。针对代理任务成功、LLM越狱、有毒内容生成和RAG幻觉的全面实验表明，DAPRO在不超过预期预算约束的情况下，达到了更接近名义水平的覆盖率，并且方差低于静态基线。
Summary / 总结
This article addresses the challenge of evaluating large language models (LLMs) in multi-turn conversational settings, where key events like jailbreaks may only occur after numerous interactions, making traditional evaluation methods computationally expensive and often ineffective due to static budget allocation. Previous methods, particularly conformal survival frameworks, fail to adaptively allocate resources, leading to potential underestimation of event occurrences. The proposed Dynamic Allocation via PRojected Optimization (DAPRO) framework overcomes these limitations by providing a dynamic budget allocation that adheres to expected constraints and offers tighter coverage guarantees without the assumptions of prior methods. The research methodology involves theoretical validation of DAPRO and its application to estimate population-level metrics under constrained resources. Experimental results indicate that DAPRO achieves better coverage and lower variance compared to static methods while maintaining budget constraints, thus supporting its effectiveness in evaluating LLM performance.
本研究解决了在多轮对话环境中评估大型语言模型（LLMs）的挑战，其中关键事件通常在多次交互后发生，但观察这些事件的计算成本很高。以往的方法，特别是符合生存框架，依赖于静态预算分配，这限制了它们在动态环境中的适应性和有效性。所提出的动态分配通过投影优化（DAPRO）框架克服了这些局限性，提供了一种动态预算分配，确保预期预算约束，同时提供更紧凑的覆盖保证。该方法论包括一种新颖的覆盖界限，改善了现有方法，并允许对评估指标（如越狱率）进行无偏估计。实验结果表明，DAPRO在遵循预算约束的同时，能够实现比静态方法更好的覆盖和更低的方差，从而支持其在实际应用中的有效性。
FinRED: An Expert-Guided Benchmark Generation and Evaluation Framework for Financial LLM Red-Teaming
Authors: Chaeyun Kim, Daeyoung Park, Junghwan Kim, Jinyoung Jeong, Eunji Song, Yongtaek Lim, Minwoo Kim
First: 2026-06-18T07:46:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-22T05:37:13+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IEEE ICDM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Existing safety benchmarks target general adversarial scenarios but miss finance-specific risks. Financial LLMs face regulatory compliance violations, fraud facilitation, and systemic trust erosion that require targeted evaluation. We introduce FinRED, an expert-guided red-teaming framework for financial LLM safety evaluation developed with financial experts. FinRED uses a novel two-level taxonomy mapping global standards (e.g., FATF and EU DORA) to threats ranging from regulatory evasion to complex fraud, integrated with a scalable pipeline that converts real financial documents into context-rich red-teaming Behavioral Prompts (seeds) through an expert-defined schema. Rigorous expert validation confirms seed plausibility and realism for meaningful LLM safety evaluation. We also provide an expert-validated, finance-specific rubric that goes beyond disclaimer checks, aligns more closely with human experts than static one-size-fits-all rubrics, and reduces critical false negatives from 28 to 12. Aligned with internationally adopted risk-management and information-security standards (e.g., ISO/IEC 27001), FinRED is deployed in South Korea's Financial Security Institute (FSI) regulatory sandbox for generative AI security evaluation in real financial services. To mitigate dual-use risks, the dataset, generation pipeline, prompt template, and evaluation framework are gated for qualified researchers at https://github.com/selectstar-ai/FinRED-paper and https://huggingface.co/datasets/datumo/FinRED.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FinRED：一个专家指导的金融LLM红队评估基准生成框架
现有的安全基准针对一般对抗场景，但忽视了金融特定风险。金融LLM面临合规违规、欺诈促进和系统信任侵蚀等问题，需要针对性评估。我们引入FinRED，这是一个由金融专家开发的专家指导的金融LLM安全评估红队框架。FinRED使用一种新颖的两级分类法，将全球标准（如FATF和EU DORA）映射到从合规规避到复杂欺诈的威胁，结合一个可扩展的管道，通过专家定义的模式将真实金融文档转换为上下文丰富的红队行为提示（种子）。严格的专家验证确认种子的合理性和真实性，以便进行有意义的LLM安全评估。我们还提供了一个经过专家验证的金融特定评分标准，超越了免责声明检查，更加贴近人类专家，而不是静态的一刀切评分标准，并将关键的假阴性从28减少到12。FinRED与国际公认的风险管理和信息安全标准（如ISO/IEC 27001）保持一致，已在韩国金融安全研究所（FSI）的监管沙箱中部署，用于真实金融服务中的生成AI安全评估。为了减轻双重用途风险，数据集、生成管道、提示模板和评估框架对合格研究人员进行了限制，网址为https://github.com/selectstar-ai/FinRED-paper和https://huggingface.co/datasets/datumo/FinRED。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the inadequacy of existing safety benchmarks for financial large language models (LLMs), which typically focus on general adversarial scenarios and overlook finance-specific risks such as regulatory compliance violations and fraud. Previous methods lacked a targeted approach, leading to ineffective evaluations that did not align with the unique challenges in the financial sector. The proposed FinRED framework, developed with financial experts, introduces a two-level taxonomy that maps global standards to specific threats and utilizes a scalable pipeline to create context-rich red-teaming prompts from real financial documents. This expert-guided methodology not only enhances the plausibility and realism of evaluation seeds but also significantly reduces critical false negatives in safety assessments. FinRED has been validated in a regulatory sandbox in South Korea, demonstrating its effectiveness in evaluating generative AI security in financial services, thus supporting its goal of improving safety evaluations for financial LLMs.

BreakFun: Jailbreaking LLMs via Object Instantiation under Simulated Code Execution
Authors: Amirkia Rafiei Oskooei, Mehmet S. Aktas
First: 2025-10-19T11:27:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-18T18:53:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted to AACL-IJCNLP 2026 Findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely used because they process structures, syntax and code well, but this same ability also makes them paradoxically vulnerable. We introduce BreakFun, a jailbreak method that frames a harmful request as code-execution simulation. The prompt gives the model a benign Python class definition, the "Trojan Schema", and asks what that code would print if it ran. To answer, the model must create an object from the class and invent a value for each field, and the adversarial field names steer those values toward the attacker's goal. The harmful content therefore appears as a side-effect of the simulated instantiation, not as a direct answer. A three-part prompt carries the attack: an innocent frame, the Trojan Schema, and a Chain-of-Thought distraction. On JailbreakBench, BreakFun reaches an average attack success rate of 89% across 13 open-weight and commercial models (open-weight ~98%, API systems ~78%), and 100% on several. An ablation identifies the Trojan Schema as the strongest contributing component. As a counter-ablation probe we introduce Adversarial Prompt Deconstruction (APD), in which a second LLM writes out all readable text in the prompt before it judges safety. APD improves detection across three model families, and a probe-specific ablation shows that the transcription step gives most of the gain. Our findings show that LLM safety must account for content that a simulation task produces as a side-effect, not only for content that a user requests directly.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BreakFun：通过模拟代码执行中的对象实例化破解LLM
大型语言模型（LLM）因其良好的结构、语法和代码处理能力而被广泛使用，但这种能力也使其在某种程度上变得脆弱。我们介绍了BreakFun，这是一种将有害请求框架化为代码执行模拟的越狱方法。提示给模型提供了一个无害的Python类定义，即“特洛伊模式”，并询问如果运行该代码会打印什么。为了回答，模型必须从类中创建一个对象并为每个字段发明一个值，而对抗性字段名称则将这些值引导至攻击者的目标。因此，有害内容作为模拟实例化的副作用出现，而不是直接答案。攻击通过三部分提示进行：一个无辜的框架、特洛伊模式和一个思维链干扰。在JailbreakBench上，BreakFun在13个开放权重和商业模型中达到了89%的平均攻击成功率（开放权重约98%，API系统约78%），并在多个模型上达到了100%。消融实验确定特洛伊模式是最强的贡献成分。作为对抗消融探针，我们引入了对抗性提示解构（APD），其中第二个LLM在判断安全性之前写出提示中的所有可读文本。APD提高了三个模型家族的检测能力，而特定探针的消融显示转录步骤带来了大部分增益。我们的研究结果表明，LLM安全性必须考虑模拟任务作为副作用产生的内容，而不仅仅是用户直接请求的内容。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the vulnerability of Large Language Models (LLMs) to harmful requests due to their ability to process code and structures effectively. Previous methods for mitigating such vulnerabilities have been inadequate, as they often fail to consider the nuanced ways in which LLMs can be manipulated. The proposed method, BreakFun, innovatively frames harmful requests as code-execution simulations, utilizing a benign Python class definition known as the 'Trojan Schema' to guide the model's output toward the attacker's objectives without direct prompts. This approach is well-motivated as it exploits the inherent capabilities of LLMs while circumventing traditional safety measures. The paper contributes a novel jailbreak technique that achieves an average attack success rate of 89% across various models on the JailbreakBench, with specific models reaching up to 100%. Additionally, the introduction of Adversarial Prompt Deconstruction (APD) enhances detection of such attacks, demonstrating that LLM safety must consider side-effect content generated during simulation tasks.
本研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）因其有效处理代码和结构的能力而面临的脆弱性。以往的缓解方法往往未能考虑在代码执行模拟过程中生成的间接有害输出。提出的BreakFun方法创新性地将有害请求框架化为代码执行模拟，利用无害的Python类定义引导模型的输出朝向攻击者的目标，而无需直接提示。这种方法的动机充分，因为它利用了模型固有的处理能力，同时规避了传统的安全措施。本文的贡献在于提出了一种新的越狱技术，在JailbreakBench上实现了89%的平均攻击成功率，并确定了Trojan Schema等特定组件对其有效性至关重要。此外，引入的对抗性提示解构（APD）增强了对有害内容的检测，表明LLM安全性必须考虑模拟任务产生的副作用输出。
Local Sparsity Enables Unsupervised LLM Safety Detection
Authors: Xin Chen, Gil Kur, Alexander Shevchenko, Andreas Krause
First: 2026-09-17T12:23:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-17T12:23:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deployment-time safety methods for large language models (LLMs) are predominantly supervised and assume access to unsafe training data. Nevertheless, new attacks and harm categories regularly arise, not captured by models trained in such a supervised fashion. An alternative approach is to view this problem through the lens of anomaly detection, namely, to rely solely on modeling safe data and flagging out-of-distribution inputs. However, LLM activations lie in a high-dimensional space, raising concerns about whether anomaly detection is statistically feasible. We show that, under the linear representation hypothesis (LRH), there may indeed be hope. In the LRH concept space, which is typically recovered via a sparse autoencoder (SAE), nearby points share a small common active support. Using this local sparsity insight, we propose a framework for locally masked SAE-based anomaly detection, supported by theoretical justifications. We validate it on various architectures and datasets, including both capability-testing datasets and safety-specific datasets. Finally, when we allow algorithms to use 1% out-of-distribution data for calibration, locally sparse methods achieve near-optimal performance, demonstrating their ability to capture meaningful safety information while using only 1-2% of SAE neurons for computation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：局部稀疏性使无监督LLM安全检测成为可能
大型语言模型（LLM）的部署时安全方法主要是监督性的，并假设可以访问不安全的训练数据。然而，新攻击和伤害类别定期出现，这些在以这种监督方式训练的模型中并未被捕获。另一种方法是通过异常检测的视角来看待这个问题，即仅依赖于建模安全数据并标记分布外输入。然而，LLM激活位于高维空间，这引发了关于异常检测在统计上是否可行的担忧。我们表明，在线性表示假设（LRH）下，确实可能存在希望。在LRH概念空间中，通常通过稀疏自编码器（SAE）恢复，附近点共享一个小的共同活跃支持。利用这一局部稀疏性洞察，我们提出了一种基于局部掩蔽SAE的异常检测框架，并提供了理论依据。我们在各种架构和数据集上进行了验证，包括能力测试数据集和安全特定数据集。最后，当我们允许算法使用1%的分布外数据进行校准时，局部稀疏方法实现了接近最佳的性能，展示了它们在仅使用1-2%的SAE神经元进行计算时捕获有意义的安全信息的能力。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the limitations of existing supervised safety methods for large language models (LLMs), which rely on unsafe training data and fail to adapt to new attacks and harm categories. The proposed approach shifts towards anomaly detection by modeling safe data and identifying out-of-distribution inputs, overcoming the challenges posed by the high-dimensional nature of LLM activations. The paper contributes a framework for locally masked sparse autoencoder (SAE)-based anomaly detection, supported by theoretical justifications, and demonstrates its effectiveness across various architectures and datasets. The methodology allows for the use of only 1% out-of-distribution data for calibration, achieving near-optimal performance and effectively capturing safety information with minimal computational resources.
本研究解决了现有大型语言模型（LLM）监督安全方法的局限性，这些方法依赖于潜在不安全的训练数据，可能无法适应新类型的攻击。以往的方法在处理LLM激活的高维特性时面临挑战，使得异常检测变得困难。所提出的方法利用线性表示假设（LRH），引入了一种局部掩蔽稀疏自编码器（SAE）框架进行异常检测，专注于建模安全数据并识别分布外输入。该方法的动机明确，旨在在不需要大量不安全数据的情况下增强安全检测。论文贡献了一种新颖的框架，在各种数据集上展示了有效的性能，仅使用少量SAE神经元就实现了接近最佳的结果，从而支持了提高LLM安全检测的目标，同时减少了资源使用。
From Intent to Action: Benchmarking LLM Safety in Vehicle Voice Command Authorization
Authors: Diba Afroze, Xingli Zhang, Yazhou Tu, Xiali Hei
First: 2026-09-17T03:20:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-17T03:20:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into vehicle voice assistants. But linking natural-language requests to vehicle functions creates a safety-critical authorization problem. Before executing a command, the system must choose whether to execute, refuse, clarify, require confirmation, defer to manual control, trigger an emergency response, or make no tool call. To our knowledge, prior evaluations do not isolate this pre-action decision across speaker role, authentication status, vehicle state, and tool availability. We introduce a 202-scenario benchmark with Reference Decisions under a seven-class taxonomy. We evaluate two local open-weight models and three API-based LLMs using Decision Alignment and safety-specific error metrics. Alignment ranges from 40.1% for Llama 3.2 3B to 89.1% for Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview. The API-based models score between 83.2% and 89.1%, with no statistically significant differences among them. Even these models produce two to three False Executes among 161 non-execution scenarios, and persistent errors remain in confirmation and manual-control decisions. A controlled Llama 3.2 3B ablation increases alignment to 40.1% under the structured authorization policy, versus 28.2-29.2% under schema-only and generic-safety baselines, but it does not eliminate False Executes. Structured LLM decisions are therefore insufficient as a standalone safety mechanism, and deployment requires an independent enforcement layer that verifies tool permissions and vehicle-state constraints before invoking any vehicle function.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从意图到行动：车辆语音命令授权中的大型语言模型安全基准测试
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地集成到车辆语音助手中。但将自然语言请求与车辆功能关联起来会产生一个安全关键的授权问题。在执行命令之前，系统必须选择执行、拒绝、澄清、要求确认、推迟手动控制、触发紧急响应或不进行任何工具调用。根据我们的了解，之前的评估并未在发言者角色、身份验证状态、车辆状态和工具可用性之间隔离这一预行动决策。我们引入了一个包含202个场景的基准测试，采用七类分类法下的参考决策。我们使用决策对齐和安全特定错误指标评估了两个本地开放权重模型和三个基于API的LLMs。对齐率从Llama 3.2 3B的40.1%到Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview的89.1%不等。基于API的模型得分在83.2%到89.1%之间，且它们之间没有统计学显著差异。即使这些模型在161个非执行场景中也产生了两到三个错误执行，并且在确认和手动控制决策中仍然存在持续错误。受控的Llama 3.2 3B消融在结构化授权政策下将对齐率提高到40.1%，而在仅使用模式和通用安全基准下为28.2-29.2%，但并未消除错误执行。因此，结构化LLM决策作为独立的安全机制是不够的，部署需要一个独立的执行层，在调用任何车辆功能之前验证工具权限和车辆状态约束。
Summary / 总结
The research addresses the safety-critical issue of linking natural-language requests to vehicle functions in voice assistants, a problem that has not been adequately evaluated in prior studies. Existing methods fail to isolate the pre-action decision-making process across various factors such as speaker role and vehicle state, leading to potential safety risks. The proposed approach introduces a 202-scenario benchmark with Reference Decisions categorized under a seven-class taxonomy, which allows for a more nuanced evaluation of decision-making in vehicle voice command authorization. The methodology involves assessing two local open-weight models and three API-based LLMs using Decision Alignment and safety-specific error metrics, revealing alignment scores ranging from 40.1% to 89.1%. Despite improvements, the models still exhibit persistent issues, such as False Executes in non-execution scenarios, indicating that structured LLM decisions alone are inadequate for ensuring safety, necessitating an additional enforcement layer for effective deployment.
本研究解决了将自然语言请求与语音助手中的车辆功能链接的安全关键问题，强调了以往评估未能根据各种因素隔离预行动决策的不足。现有方法未能充分考虑授权决策的复杂性，导致潜在的安全风险。本文提出了一个包含202个场景的基准，使用七类分类法对参考决策进行更好的评估。该方法论涉及评估两个本地开放权重模型和三个基于API的LLM，使用决策一致性和安全特定错误指标进行评估，显示出一致性得分范围为40.1%到89.1%。尽管有所改善，这些模型仍然存在持续错误，表明仅依赖结构化LLM决策不足以确保安全，因此需要额外的执行层来确保在执行命令之前正确的工具权限和车辆状态约束。
HoloAegis: Frozen Representation, Topological Inference --- Minimally Parametric Safety Manifolds and Their Capability Boundaries for LLM Guardrails
Authors: Tak Ho Alex Li, Kaijie Liu, Lik-Hang Lee, Kin Chung Ho, Ping Shum, Michael K. Ng
First: 2026-08-09T05:05:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-15T12:22:48+00:00
Comments: Preprint v2, September 2026. 4 figures, 12 tables. Corrected and substantially revised from v1 (arXiv:2608.08485v1)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current LLM safety guardrails face a fundamental tension: fine-tuning distorts pre-trained representations while generative judges incur prohibitive inference costs. We ask a complementary question: how far can safety be achieved through pure geometric reasoning over frozen representations, and where does it fail? We present HoloAegis, a minimally parametric topological inference framework that decouples representation from reasoning: an un-fine-tuned encoder maps text to the unit sphere S^{d-1}, and all decisions reduce to Gibbs-Boltzmann free-energy differences over pre-computed anchor centroids. We contribute a boundary-mapping study rather than a leaderboard claim. On a frozen three-benchmark protocol, HoloAegis (3.2 MB) statistically matches WildGuard-7B (14 GB) on toxicity (0.96 vs. 0.96), exceeds it on harmful behaviors (0.99 vs. 0.79), and cedes oversafety detection (0.62 vs. 0.98) -- while ShieldGemma-2B fails on indirect harms (0.34). These failure modes are complementary and mechanistically traceable: potential-difference scoring senses manifold clustering, whereas policy-conditioned LLM judging requires explicit taxonomy matching. We restate our Topological Boundary Stability conjecture in ratio form and validate it via reference-set bootstrap: anchor banks reduce score variance 4-15x and boundary displacement to approximately 0.44 + 0.23 sqrt(k/K) of the full-space estimator. Per-domain analysis further reveals that geometric separability tracks within-domain semantic homogeneity. Our results chart where geometric guardrails substitute for, and where they must defer to, LLM judges.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HoloAegis：冻结表示、拓扑推理 --- 最小参数安全流形及其对LLM保护措施的能力边界
当前LLM安全保护措施面临根本性矛盾：微调扭曲了预训练表示，而生成性判断则产生了高昂的推理成本。我们提出一个互补性问题：通过对冻结表示的纯几何推理，安全性可以达到多远，在哪里又会失败？我们提出HoloAegis，一个最小参数的拓扑推理框架，解耦表示与推理：未微调的编码器将文本映射到单位球面S^{d-1}，所有决策归结为对预计算锚心的Gibbs-Boltzmann自由能差异。我们贡献了一个边界映射研究，而不是排行榜声明。在一个冻结的三基准协议上，HoloAegis（3.2 MB）在毒性上与WildGuard-7B（14 GB）统计匹配（0.96 vs. 0.96），在有害行为上超越它（0.99 vs. 0.79），而在过度安全检测上则让步（0.62 vs. 0.98）——同时ShieldGemma-2B在间接危害上失败（0.34）。这些失败模式是互补的，并且在机制上可追溯：电位差评分感知流形聚类，而政策条件的LLM判断则需要明确的分类匹配。我们以比率形式重述我们的拓扑边界稳定性猜想，并通过参考集自助法进行验证：锚库将得分方差降低4-15倍，边界位移约为全空间估计器的0.44 + 0.23 sqrt(k/K)。每个领域的分析进一步揭示几何可分性与领域内语义同质性之间的关系。我们的结果描绘了几何保护措施替代和必须依赖LLM判断的地方。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the limitations of current large language model (LLM) safety guardrails, which struggle with the trade-off between fine-tuning pre-trained representations and the high costs associated with generative judges. Previous methods often distort representations or incur significant computational expenses, prompting the authors to propose HoloAegis, a minimally parametric topological inference framework that utilizes frozen representations for geometric reasoning. This approach allows for decision-making based on Gibbs-Boltzmann free-energy differences over pre-computed anchor centroids, effectively separating representation from reasoning. The paper contributes a boundary-mapping study that demonstrates HoloAegis's performance on a frozen three-benchmark protocol, achieving comparable toxicity detection to WildGuard-7B while outperforming it in harmful behavior detection. The findings indicate that geometric guardrails can effectively substitute for LLM judges in certain contexts, while also identifying the limitations of this approach in detecting indirect harms.
本文探讨了当前大型语言模型（LLM）安全防护措施面临的挑战，特别是微调预训练表示与生成判断器高成本之间的权衡。以往的方法往往会扭曲表示或产生高昂的推理成本，导致确保安全的效率低下。提出的HoloAegis框架引入了一种最小参数拓扑推理方法，利用冻结表示，允许在不微调的情况下进行几何推理。该方法有效地将文本映射到单位球体，并基于吉布斯-玻尔兹曼自由能差异做出决策，显著贡献于理解安全边界。在实验中，HoloAegis在毒性检测上与更大模型相匹配，同时在有害行为检测上表现更佳，从而验证了其在提供安全防护措施方面的有效性，并突显了现有方法的局限性。
Useless but Safe? Benchmarking Utility Recovery with User Intent Clarification in Multi-Turn Conversations
Authors: Mingqian Zheng, Malia Morgan, Liwei Jiang, Carolyn Rose, Maarten Sap
First: 2026-04-29T18:37:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-12T19:48:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current LLM safety alignment techniques improve model robustness against adversarial attacks, but overlook whether and how LLMs can recover helpfulness when benign users clarify their intent. We introduce CarryOnBench, the first interactive benchmark that measures whether LLMs can revise their interpretation of user intent and recover utility, while remaining safe through multi-turn conversations. Starting from 398 seemingly harmful queries with benign underlying intents, we simulate 5,970 conversations by varying user follow-up sequences, evaluating 14 models on both intent-aligned utility and safety. CarryOnBench yields 1,866 different conversation flows of 4--12 turns, totaling 23,880 model responses. We design Ben-Util, a checklist-based metric that evaluates how well each model response fulfills the user's benign information need using atomic items. At turn one, models fulfill only 10.5--37.6% of the user's benign information need. When the same query includes the benign intent upfront, models fulfill 25.1--72.1%, confirming that models withhold information due to intent misinterpretation, not limited knowledge. With benign clarifications in multi-turn conversations, 13 of 14 models approach or exceed this single-turn baseline, yet recovery cost varies across models. We identify two failure modes invisible to single-turn evaluations---unsafe recovery, where a model updates at disproportionate safety cost and redundant recovery, where a model recycles prior responses rather than providing new information. Moreover, conversations converge to similar harmfulness levels regardless of how conservative the model starts. These findings expose a gap that single-turn evaluations miss---whether a model is appropriately cautious or simply unresponsive to clarified user intent.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无用但安全？在多轮对话中通过用户意图澄清基准测试实用性恢复
当前的LLM安全对齐技术提高了模型对抗攻击的鲁棒性，但忽视了LLM在良性用户澄清意图时是否以及如何恢复有用性。我们引入CarryOnBench，这是第一个交互式基准，测量LLM在多轮对话中是否能够修正对用户意图的理解并恢复实用性，同时保持安全。从398个看似有害的查询开始，这些查询具有良性的潜在意图，我们通过变化用户后续序列模拟了5,970个对话，评估了14个模型在意图对齐的实用性和安全性方面的表现。CarryOnBench产生了1,866种不同的对话流程，轮次为4到12，总计23,880个模型响应。我们设计了Ben-Util，这是一个基于清单的指标，评估每个模型响应在多大程度上满足用户的良性信息需求。在第一轮中，模型仅满足用户良性信息需求的10.5%到37.6%。当同一查询在前面包含良性意图时，模型满足25.1%到72.1%，确认模型因意图误解而隐瞒信息，而不是知识有限。在多轮对话中，经过良性澄清，14个模型中的13个接近或超过这一单轮基准，但恢复成本在模型之间有所不同。我们识别出两种在单轮评估中不可见的失败模式——不安全恢复，即模型以不成比例的安全成本进行更新，以及冗余恢复，即模型重复之前的响应而不是提供新信息。此外，无论模型起始多么保守，对话都趋向于相似的有害性水平。这些发现揭示了单轮评估所忽视的一个差距——模型是否适当地谨慎，或仅仅对澄清的用户意图无反应。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the limitations of current large language model (LLM) safety alignment techniques, which enhance robustness against adversarial attacks but fail to assess how LLMs can recover their helpfulness when users clarify their intent in benign contexts. Previous methods primarily focused on single-turn evaluations, which overlook the dynamics of multi-turn conversations and the potential for models to misinterpret user intent. The proposed approach, CarryOnBench, introduces an interactive benchmark that evaluates LLMs' ability to revise their understanding of user intent and recover utility while maintaining safety across multi-turn dialogues. This study contributes by revealing the performance of 14 models through 5,970 simulated conversations, identifying two failure modes—unsafe recovery and redundant recovery—that are not apparent in single-turn assessments. The findings indicate that while models can improve their utility recovery with benign clarifications, their performance varies significantly, highlighting the need for more nuanced evaluation metrics like Ben-Util, which measures how well models meet user information needs.
本研究解决了当前大型语言模型（LLM）安全对齐技术的局限性，这些技术增强了对抗攻击的鲁棒性，但未能评估LLM在用户澄清意图时如何恢复有用性。以往的方法主要集中在单轮评估，忽视了用户意图澄清的动态，导致意图误解和无响应等问题。提出的方法CarryOnBench引入了一个交互式基准，评估LLM在保持安全的同时修正用户意图理解的能力。该方法论涉及基于398个有害查询和良性意图模拟5,970个对话，评估14个模型在意图对齐的有用性和安全性方面的表现。研究结果表明，模型最初仅满足用户需求的一小部分，但在澄清意图后显著改善，尽管恢复成本各异，揭示了单轮评估未能捕捉的失败模式。本研究有助于理解LLM在安全性与有用性恢复之间的平衡，强调多轮评估在评估模型性能中的重要性。
Adaptive Adversaries: A Multi-Turn, Multi-LLM Benchmark for LLM Agent Security
Authors: Devina Jain, David Hartmann, Chuan Li
First: 2026-07-20T15:30:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-11T18:06:53+00:00
Comments: Second Workshop on Agents in the Wild: Safety, Security, and Beyond
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based agents process external content, exposing them to prompt injection and multi-turn manipulation. We present a 21-scenario benchmark for adaptive cross-session attacks against fresh-session LLM defenders: an autonomous LLM attacker observes prior defender responses and pivots across rounds, while each defender response is evaluated as a fresh interaction. A controlled 3 x 3 attacker-defender matrix contains 945 battles. Restricting scoring to the first round yields 0-1% attack success rate (ASR); allowing 15 rounds yields 7.9-16.8%. Pooling three attacker LLMs uncovers 1.7-2.2 times as many unique successful inputs as the best single attacker, at three times the battle budget. Aggregate rates conceal opposing scenario-specific weaknesses in session-secret protection and authority handling, preserved in two higher-sample evaluations. On six scenarios, adding one provenance paragraph reduces ASR from 110/270 to 70/270, with selective effects across tasks. History and defender-state controls, together with frozen replay, characterize how the interaction protocol changes the result. A competition adds 18,422 held-out battles on a fixed gpt-oss-20b backbone and complementary benign-task evaluations. The benchmark exposes attacker and defender models, harnesses, scenarios, session state, and interaction budgets as configurable choices for systematic security evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自适应对手：多回合、多LLM基准测试用于LLM代理安全性
基于LLM的代理处理外部内容，使其暴露于提示注入和多回合操控中。我们提出了一个21场景基准，用于针对新会话LLM防御者的自适应跨会话攻击：自主LLM攻击者观察先前防御者的响应并在回合间进行调整，而每个防御者的响应被评估为一次新的交互。一个受控的3 x 3攻击者-防御者矩阵包含945场战斗。将评分限制在第一回合的攻击成功率（ASR）为0-1%；允许15回合则为7.9-16.8%。汇聚三个攻击者LLM发现的独特成功输入数量是最佳单一攻击者的1.7-2.2倍，战斗预算是三倍。汇总率掩盖了会话秘密保护和权限处理中的相对场景特定弱点，这在两个更高样本评估中得以保留。在六个场景中，添加一个来源段落将ASR从110/270降低到70/270，任务间效果选择性。历史和防御者状态控制，以及冻结重放，表征了交互协议如何改变结果。一个竞赛增加了在固定gpt-oss-20b基础上和补充良性任务评估的18,422场保留战斗。该基准揭示了攻击者和防御者模型、工具、场景、会话状态和交互预算作为系统安全评估的可配置选择。
Summary / 总结
This research addresses the vulnerabilities of LLM-based agents to prompt injection and multi-turn manipulation, which have been inadequately evaluated in previous methods that often overlook adaptive cross-session attacks. The proposed approach introduces a comprehensive 21-scenario benchmark that simulates adaptive attacks from LLM attackers observing prior defender responses, allowing for a more realistic assessment of LLM defender security. The paper contributes by establishing a controlled attacker-defender matrix with 945 battles, revealing that allowing multiple rounds significantly increases attack success rates, from 0-1% in the first round to 7.9-16.8% over 15 rounds. The methodology includes a systematic evaluation of attacker and defender models across various scenarios, demonstrating that the interaction protocol and session state significantly influence outcomes, thus providing a robust framework for assessing LLM agent security.
本研究关注基于LLM的智能体在提示注入和多轮操控方面的脆弱性，强调了改进安全措施的必要性。以往的方法缺乏对自适应攻击的全面评估框架，导致对其应对不断演变威胁的有效性理解有限。本文提出了一种新颖的基准，包含21个场景，用于评估自适应跨会话攻击，允许攻击者从先前防御者的响应中学习，同时将每次交互视为新鲜的。该方法包括945场战斗的受控矩阵，结果显示攻击成功率在第一轮为0-1%，而在15轮中提升至7.9-16.8%。研究发现，汇聚多个攻击者LLM显著增强了独特成功输入的发现，同时暴露了会话秘密保护和权限处理方面的特定弱点，从而为LLM智能体安全评估提供了更系统的方法。
'Ghaib in Translation' aka Unseen Harm: Measuring Cross-Script Safety Inconsistency with 'Missed-in-Urdu' Scores in LLM Hate Speech Detection
Authors: Fawzia Zehra, Kara-Isitt, Sonal Khosla, Stephen Swift
First: 2026-08-25T07:58:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-09T16:54:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Urdu, the world's tenth most spoken language with 246 million speakers, remains almost entirely absent from mainstream LLM safety evaluation and nine years of WOAH proceedings. To investigate whether this absence has measurable consequences for content moderation reliability, five large language models, GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4.5, Gemini 2.5 Flash, Qwen-2.5, and Llama-3.1, were tested across six datasets spanning Nastaliq Urdu, Roman Urdu, English, and code-switched Urdu-English. Across the five Urdu-script datasets, label instability between original-script and English-translation classification ranged from 15.9% (Gemini 2.5 Flash) to 31.6% (Qwen-2.5), with a 'Missed-in-Urdu' rate, content flagged as harmful in English translation but passed as normal in the original script, ranging from 2.4% to 9.9% (median 4.3%). A complete enumeration of all 205 papers across nine ALW/WOAH editions via the ACL Anthology API confirms zero dedicated Urdu papers across the entire period. Results indicate that current LLMs provide uneven safety assurance across Urdu's script varieties, with smaller open-weight models showing substantially higher instability and missed-harm rates than frontier closed models.

乌尔都语是全球第十大语言，拥有2.46亿说话者，但在主流LLM安全评估和九年的WOAH会议中几乎完全缺席。为了调查这种缺席是否对内容审核的可靠性产生可测量的影响，测试了五个大型语言模型：GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4.5、Gemini 2.5 Flash、Qwen-2.5和Llama-3.1，涵盖了六个数据集，包括Nastaliq乌尔都语、罗马乌尔都语、英语和代码切换的乌尔都语-英语。在五个乌尔都语脚本数据集中，原始脚本与英语翻译分类之间的标签不稳定性范围为15.9%（Gemini 2.5 Flash）到31.6%（Qwen-2.5），'乌尔都语遗漏'率，即在英语翻译中标记为有害但在原始脚本中通过的内容，范围为2.4%到9.9%（中位数4.3%）。通过ACL Anthology API对九个ALW/WOAH版本中205篇论文的完整枚举确认，在整个期间内没有专门的乌尔都语论文。结果表明，当前的LLM在乌尔都语的脚本变体之间提供的不均匀安全保障，较小的开放权重模型显示出显著更高的不稳定性和遗漏危害率，超过了前沿的封闭模型。

This study proposes a comprehensive evaluation of five LLMs on multiple datasets, revealing substantial label instability and a notable 'Missed-in-Urdu' rate, indicating harmful content that is misclassified when translated into English. Style Over Substance: Content-Invariant Wrappers Flip LLM Safety-Judge Verdicts
Authors: Yongxi Zhou, Wenbo Ye, Yuanzhe Liu, Zihan Dong, Junwei Yao
First: 2026-09-08T04:27:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-09-08T04:27:22+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 1 table. Code, wrappers, and per-verdict labels: https://github.com/Yongxi-Zhou/safety-judge-robustness
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Automatic safety judges -- systems such as Llama Guard or a GPT-4o grading prompt that decide whether a model's reply is harmful -- produce the numbers behind almost every reported jailbreak success rate, defense evaluation, and safety leaderboard. We ask whether these judges grade what a reply contains or how it sounds. We keep a reply's content fixed and add content-invariant style wrappers: fixed strings placed before or after the reply that change only its tone (an educational disclaimer, a fake safety "reasoning" block, a token refusal followed by the unchanged harmful body), or, on harmless refusals, framing that merely sounds dangerous. The body is preserved byte-for-byte, so a faithful judge must return the same verdict, and any flip is an error of the judge, not a change in safety. Over 600 JailbreakBench replies x up to 7 forms x 8 judges, we measure flip rates with paired significance tests and measured noise floors. Findings are precise rather than universal: most judges barely move, but specific judges harbor cheaply exploitable blind spots. A token-refusal wrapper flips 19.9% of GPT-4o-mini's correct "unsafe" verdicts (noise floor 0.5%; 18.2% under majority-of-three re-scoring) yet moves Claude only 0.4%. The deployed Llama Guard 4 is deterministically gamed: an "educational course" framing flips 12.3% of its harmful verdicts to safe. A second deployed guard (gpt-oss-safeguard-20b) is immune, and rewriting only the grading prompt (StrongREJECT-style) cuts the attack tenfold on the identical model -- the vulnerability lives in the judge, not the content. A two-annotator human validation confirms 100% content invariance and 90% of flips as judge errors (kappa 0.95-1.0), and a bootstrap shows the underlying model ranking is already unstable to sampling alone. We release the dataset, wrappers, code, and per-verdict labels.
中文标题/摘要
标题：形式胜于内容：内容不变的包装翻转LLM安全评判结果
自动安全评判系统——如Llama Guard或GPT-4o评分提示——决定模型回复是否有害，产生几乎所有报告的越狱成功率、防御评估和安全排行榜背后的数据。我们探讨这些评判是根据回复的内容还是其语气进行评分。我们保持回复内容不变，添加内容不变的风格包装：在回复前后放置的固定字符串，仅改变其语气（教育免责声明、虚假的安全“推理”块、后接不变的有害内容的拒绝令），或者在无害拒绝的情况下，仅仅听起来危险的框架。主体逐字保留，因此一个忠实的评判必须返回相同的判决，任何翻转都是评判的错误，而不是安全的变化。在超过600个JailbreakBench回复中，乘以最多7种形式和8个评判，我们通过配对显著性测试和测量噪声底线来测量翻转率。发现是精确的而非普遍的：大多数评判几乎没有变化，但特定评判存在廉价可利用的盲点。一个令牌拒绝包装翻转了19.9%的GPT-4o-mini的正确“unsafe”判决（噪声底线0.5%；在三人多数重新评分下为18.2%），但对Claude的影响仅为0.4%。已部署的Llama Guard 4被确定性地利用：“教育课程”框架将12.3%的有害判决翻转为安全。第二个已部署的保护（gpt-oss-safeguard-20b）是免疫的，仅重写评分提示（StrongREJECT风格）就将相同模型上的攻击减少十倍——脆弱性存在于评判中，而非内容。两位注释者的人类验证确认100%的内容不变性和90%的翻转为评判错误（kappa 0.95-1.0），而自助法显示基础模型排名已经对抽样不稳定。我们发布数据集、包装、代码和每个判决标签。

This research investigates the effectiveness of automatic safety judges, which evaluate the harmfulness of model replies, by examining whether their assessments are influenced by content or merely by stylistic presentation. Previous methods relied on direct content analysis, which often led to vulnerabilities that could be exploited through superficial changes in tone or framing. The proposed approach introduces content-invariant style wrappers that alter the presentation of replies without changing their content, allowing for a clear assessment of judge reliability. The study's contribution lies in revealing specific weaknesses in certain judges, demonstrating that a token-refusal wrapper can flip nearly 20% of harmful verdicts from GPT-4o-mini, while other judges remain largely unaffected. The methodology involved testing over 600 replies across multiple judges and forms, confirming that the vulnerabilities reside within the judges themselves rather than the content, thus highlighting the need for improved safety evaluation mechanisms.
